interface X0
    ip-assignment LAN static
        ip 192.168.168.168 netmask 255.255.255.0
        no gateway
        exit

    comment "tz 300 168 Default LAN"
    management https
    management ping
    no management snmp
    management ssh
    no user-login http
    no user-login https
    https-redirect
    link-speed auto-negotiate
    mac default
    flow-reporting
    no multicast
    no cos-8021p
    no exclude-route
    no routed-mode
    mtu 1500
    exit

interface X1
    ip-assignment WAN static
        ip 88.88.88.88 netmask 255.255.255.0
        dns primary 88.88.88.53
        dns secondary 88.88.88.253
        no dns tertiary
        gateway 88.88.88.87
        exit

    comment "Default WAN 88 gw 87 dns 53 253"
    management https
    management ping
    no management snmp
    no management ssh
    no user-login http
    no user-login https
    https-redirect
    link-speed auto-negotiate
    mac default
    flow-reporting
    no multicast
    no cos-8021p
    no exclude-route
    mtu 1500
    fragment-packets
    no ignore-df-bit
    no send-icmp-fragmentation
    exit

    ip-assignment LAN portshield X0
    link-speed auto-negotiate
    mac default
    no exclude-route
    mtu 1500
    exit

interface X4
    ip-assignment LAN portshield X0
    link-speed auto-negotiate
    mac default
    no exclude-route
    mtu 1500
    exit

interface X2 vlan 88
    ip-assignment LAN static
        ip 192.168.88.1 netmask 255.255.255.0
        gateway 192.168.88.254
        exit

    comment "vlan x2 88 gw 254"
    management https
    no management ping
    no management snmp
    management ssh
    no user-login http
    no user-login https
    https-redirect
    mac default
    flow-reporting
    no multicast
    no exclude-route
    no default-8021p-cos
    no routed-mode
    mtu 1500
    exit

interface ipv6 X0
    ip-assignment static
        ip ::
        prefix-length 64
        no advertise subnet-prefix

        router-advertisement
            no enable
            interval min 200
            interval max 600
            no link-mtu
            no reachable-time
            no retransmit-timer
            current-hop-limit 64
            router lifetime 1800
            router preference medium
            no managed
            no other-config
            exit
        exit

    ipv6-traffic
    no listen-router-advertisement
    duplicate-address-detection-transmits 1
    reachable-time 30
    max ndp-size 1200
    management https
    management ping
    no management snmp
    no user-login http
    no user-login https
    https-redirect
    exit

interface ipv6 X1
    ip-assignment static
        ip ::
        prefix-length 64
        dns primary ::
        dns secondary ::
        dns ternary ::
        gateway ::
        no advertise subnet-prefix

        router-advertisement
            no enable
            interval min 200
            interval max 600
            no link-mtu
            no reachable-time
            no retransmit-timer
            current-hop-limit 64
            router lifetime 1800
            router preference medium
            no managed
            no other-config
            exit
        exit

    ipv6-traffic
    listen-router-advertisement
    no stateless-address-autoconfig
    duplicate-address-detection-transmits 1
    reachable-time 30
    max ndp-size 128
    no management https
    no management ping
    no management snmp
    no user-login http
    no user-login https
    exit
